 {"content": "**Repository Summary**\n- **Purpose**: Odoo 19 custom addons to manage a sports federation (clubs, teams, seasons, tournaments, rules, officiating, rosters, results, public site, reporting).\n- **Location**: All addons live under the `odoo/` folder as individual modules prefixed with `sports_federation_`.\n-- **Primary docs**: See [odoo/CONTEXT.md](odoo/CONTEXT.md#L1), [odoo/TECHNICAL_NOTE.md](odoo/TECHNICAL_NOTE.md#L1), and the Workflows in [odoo/_workflows/WORKFLOW_TOURNAMENT_LIFECYCLE.md](odoo/_workflows/WORKFLOW_TOURNAMENT_LIFECYCLE.md#L1).\n\n**Architecture & Patterns**\n- **Module split**: `sports_federation_base` (master data), `sports_federation_tournament` (tournaments, matches, participants), `sports_federation_format / sports_federation_scheduling` (service/wizards), plus domain modules (`people`, `rules`, `officiating`, `rosters`, `result_control`, `public_site`, `reporting`, `finance_bridge`, `venues`, `discipline`, `standings`, ...).\n- **Model naming**: Domain models use the `federation.` prefix (e.g. `federation.player`, `federation.match`).\n- **Common patterns**: `ir.sequence` for identifiers, `state` selection fields for workflows, wizards for batch operations (under `wizards/`), `controllers/` for portal/website endpoints, and `security/` for groups/record rules/ACLs.\n\n**Key Files & Where To Look**\n- **High-level context**: [odoo/CONTEXT.md](odoo/CONTEXT.md#L1)\n- **Technical architecture**: [odoo/TECHNICAL_NOTE.md](odoo/TECHNICAL_NOTE.md#L1)\n-- **Workflows (authoritative behavioural spec)**: [odoo/_workflows/WORKFLOW_TOURNAMENT_LIFECYCLE.md](odoo/_workflows/WORKFLOW_TOURNAMENT_LIFECYCLE.md#L1) and the other files inside `odoo/_workflows`.\n- **Module READMEs**: each module may have a `README.md` under `odoo/<module>/README.md` with implementation notes and expectations.\n-- **Install logs / historical notes**: `_logs/` contains installation and setup notes per module.\n\n**Development Guidelines (how to implement changes)**\n- **Add/modify a model**: create/modify `models/*.py`, export package in `models/__init__.py`, add access rules in `security/ir.model.access.csv`, add any initial `data/*.xml` and register them in `__manifest__.py` `data` or `demo` lists, add views in `views/*.xml`, update `menu_items.xml` as needed, and add tests under `tests/`.\n- **Add a wizard or service**: put transient models under `wizards/` and views under `views/` (manifest `data`), keep service logic in modules like `sports_federation_format / sports_federation_scheduling` (no persistent models if not required).\n- **Add a controller / portal flow**: use `controllers/` and add templates under `views/` or `templates/`; respect portal record rules (see `sports_federation_portal`).\n- **Security**: always add or update `security/ir.model.access.csv` and any `security/*.xml` groups/rules for new models or new endpoints.\n- **Manifest hygiene**: update `__manifest__.py` `depends`, `data`, and `version` fields when introducing DB changes or new data files.\n\n**Testing & Local Run**\n- **Where tests live**: `odoo/<module>/tests/` when present (some modules include unit/functional tests).\n- **Run module tests**: use the Odoo test runner for module-level tests (e.g. `odoo-bin -d <db> -i <module> --test-enable`) or your established local development workflow.\n- **Release sanity**: include at least one focused test for new business logic (especially schedule generation, result pipeline, and state transitions).\n\n**PR Checklist (required before merge)**\n- **Tests**: Add/adjust tests covering the new behaviour.\n- **Security**: Update `security/ir.model.access.csv` and add group/record-rule XML if necessary.\n- **Manifest**: Add new data files to `__manifest__.py` `data` list and ensure `depends` are correct.\n-- **Docs**: Update or add a short README in the module and, if behaviour changes, update the relevant `odoo/_workflows` file.\n- **Small, focused commits**: Prefer atomic commits (one logical change per commit) and descriptive commit messages.\n\n**How Copilot Should Help (recommended prompts & behaviour)**\n- **Primary rule**: Prefer conservative, minimal changes that keep module boundaries intact. Avoid touching unrelated modules unless absolutely required and explain why.\n- **When asked to implement a model change**: return a plan and a single patch that includes: the `models/<file>.py` change, `models/__init__.py` export, `security/ir.model.access.csv` entry, `views/*.xml` (if UI), `__manifest__.py` update, and a new test under `tests/` that exercises the main logic.\n- **When asked to add a wizard or generator**: create a transient model in `wizards/`, a wizard view, and a small deterministic example in tests that demonstrates expected output (e.g. round-robin schedule for 4 participants).\n- **When asked about business rules or workflows**: consult and reference the Workflow docs in `odoo/_workflows` and the `TECHNICAL_NOTE.md` to ensure behaviour matches the documented flows.\n\n**Example prompts you can give Copilot in this repo**\n- **Add a model**: \"Add a new persistent model `federation.referee` with fields `name`, `certification_level`, `active`; add ACLs, a basic tree/form view, and unit tests. Show me the patch with files changed.\"\n- **Create a wizard**: \"Create a `round_robin_wizard` that generates matches for a stage; include wizard model, view, manifest update, and a unit test verifying match count for 4 teams.\"\n- **Fix workflow behaviour**: \"Investigate why standings recompute includes contested results; propose a minimal fix and tests to prevent contested results from being counted.\"\n\n**Notes & Expectations**\n- **Authoritative sources**: the `_workflows` documents and `TECHNICAL_NOTE.md` are the source of truth for business behaviour—use them before changing workflows.\n- **Keep modules decoupled**: the architecture intentionally separates `base`, `tournament`, and the `competition_engine` service; prefer adding features in the appropriate module.\n- **Ask clarifying questions**: if a requested feature impacts tournament rules, scheduling, or publication flows, ask which workflow (`_workflows/*.md`) should govern the behaviour.\n\n**Documentation Maintenance (required)**\n\n- **Always update docs**: For any code, data model, view, workflow, or behaviour change, update the relevant documentation files in this repository as part of the same change set (or in a linked follow-up PR). At minimum, consider updating `odoo/TECHNICAL_NOTE.md`, `odoo/CONTEXT.md`, the module-level `README.md` under the affected `odoo/<module>/`, and any affected workflow files under `odoo/_workflows/`.\n- **Doc scope**: Document API/ORM changes, new models, new fields, migration notes (DB changes), public website impacts, and any user-facing behaviour changes. Include brief examples or CLI commands for running tests or sample data when useful.\n- **PR checklist enforcement**: Fail-fast — do not open a PR merging code that introduces behavioural or schema changes without documentation entries. If you cannot edit docs yourself (e.g., lacking domain clarity), leave a clear TODO in the code and notify repository maintainers.\n- **Why**: Keeping documentation current ensures maintainability, reduces onboarding friction, and prevents accidental regressions in workflows that rely on textual contracts (workflows, assumptions, and operational steps).\n\n", "content_length": 7327, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": ".github/copilot-instructions.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "7e616893749fb5ae32de33c88010309516272742120b3b28cec305c140409842", "size_bytes": 7331}

 {"content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Validate manifests, explicit assets, Python imports and model ACL coverage.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\nimport ast\nimport csv\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport re\nimport sys\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nerrors: list[str] = []\naddons = sorted(path.parent for path in ROOT.glob(\"*/__manifest__.py\"))\n\n\ndef manifest_for(addon: Path) -> dict:\n    try:\n        value = ast.literal_eval(\n            (addon / \"__manifest__.py\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        )\n    except (OSError, SyntaxError, ValueError) as exc:\n        errors.append(f\"{addon.name}: manifest cannot be parsed: {exc}\")\n        return {}\n    if not isinstance(value, dict):\n        errors.append(f\"{addon.name}: manifest is not a dictionary\")\n        return {}\n    return value\n\n\ndef concrete_models(addon: Path) -> set[str]:\n    result = set()\n    for path in addon.rglob(\"*.py\"):\n        if \"tests\" in path.parts or \"migrations\" in path.parts:\n            continue\n        try:\n            tree = ast.parse(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"), filename=str(path))\n        except (OSError, SyntaxError, UnicodeError) as exc:\n            errors.append(f\"{path.relative_to(ROOT)}: cannot parse: {exc}\")\n            continue\n        for node in tree.body:\n            if not isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):\n                continue\n            base_names = {\n                base.attr if isinstance(base, ast.Attribute) else base.id\n                for base in  {"content": "# Competition Workflow in the User Interface\n\nOwner: Federation Competition Operations\nLast verified against menu XML: 2026-09-04\n\n## Purpose\n\nThis guide replaces conceptual module names with the menus that actually exist in the **Federation** application. A menu can be hidden if the current user lacks the required role or record access.\n\n## Actual backend menu map\n\n```text\nFederation\n├── Setup\n│   ├── Core Records\n│   │   ├── Clubs\n│   │   └── Teams\n│   ├── Seasons\n│   ├── Season Registrations\n│   └── Competition Setup\n│       ├── Competition Templates\n│       ├── Season Competitions\n│       └── Rules & Policies\n├── Competition Workflow\n│   ├── Create Competition\n│   ├── Competition Overview\n│   ├── Registration Desk\n│   ├── Format Studio\n│   ├── Format Templates\n│   ├── Calendar Planner\n│   ├── Schedule Planner\n│   ├── Schedule Review Queue\n│   ├── Match-Day Control\n│   └── Technical Records\n├── Planning\n│   ├── Operational Action Queue\n│   └── Advanced Records\n│       ├── Divisions & Tournaments\n│       ├── Matches\n│       └── Tournament Structure\n├── Match Day\n│   ├── Match Sheets\n│   ├── Venues\n│   ├── Officiating\n│   └── Discipline\n├── Publication\n│   ├── Standings\n│   ├── Approved Schedules\n│   └── Schedule Publications\n└── Administration\n    ├── Reporting\n    │   └── Operational Health\n    ├── Retention and Recovery\n    ├── Retention Evidence\n    └── Operational Job Health\n```\n\nThere is no backend menu named **Competitions**, **Registrations**, **Scheduling**, **Schedule Approval**, **Results**, or **Competition Engine**. Those are concepts or addon names, not current navigation labels.\n\n## Actual club portal links\n\n```text\nMy Account\n├── Overview                 /my/competitions\n├── Action items             /my/action-items\n├── My teams                 /my/teams\n├── Season registrations     /my/season-registrations\n├── Match day                /my/match-day\n├── Officials                /my/referee-duties\n└── Results                  /my/results\n```\n\n## Complete workflow and recovery\n\n### 1. Prepare reusable setup\n\nUse:\n\n```text\nFederation > Setup > Seasons\nFederation > Setup > Competition Setup > Competition Templates\nFederation > Setup > Competition Setup > Rules & Policies > Rule Sets & Policies\nFederation > Setup > Core Records > Clubs\nFederation > Setup > Core Records > Teams\n```\n\nCreate the season, reusable competition definition, rule set, clubs, and teams. Points, tie-break, eligibility, and qualification components are below **Rules & Policies > Advanced Components**.\n\n**Recovery:** Edit unused records. Archive records that already have history. Do not delete referenced seasons, competitions, clubs, teams, or rule sets merely to restart a workflow.\n\n### 2. Create the season competition\n\nPreferred menu:\n\n```text\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Create Competition\n```\n\nThe wizard selects the setup template, competition template, season, dates, optional rule set, registration dates, divisions, and responsibilities. It creates the season competition, divisions, registration windows, and role assignments, then opens the season competition.\n\nInspect the records through:\n\n```text\nFederation > Setup > Competition Setup > Season Competitions\nFederation > Planning > Advanced Records > Divisions & Tournaments\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Competition Overview\n```\n\n**Recovery:** Correct newly created records before clubs submit. There is no generic **Undo Competition Wizard** button. If downstream entries, fixtures, schedules, results, or publications exist, preserve the original records and create controlled replacements.\n\n### 3. Open registration and accept entries\n\nBackend:\n\n```text\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Registration Desk\n```\n\nClub portal:\n\n```text\nMy Account > Season registrations\n```\n\nUse **Open Registration** on a draft registration window. Clubs prepare and submit entries. Managers review them in the Registration Desk.\n\n**Recovery:** Use **Close Registration** to stop submissions. A submitted entry can be withdrawn by the club or returned by the manager with a reason. A returned entry can be corrected and resubmitted. Rejection remains historical evidence.\n\n### 4. Finalize participants\n\nMenu:\n\n```text\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Registration Desk\n```\n\nClose the registration window and press **Finalize Participants**. The finalized participant set feeds the format structure.\n\n**Recovery:** Correct entries before finalization. If a finalized set is wrong and nothing depends on it, create a corrected set through the registration workflow. If fixtures or schedules exist, create a successor structure or schedule revision instead of mutating published history.\n\n### 5. Configure and generate the format\n\nMenus:\n\n```text\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Format Templates\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Format Studio\n```\n\n**Format Templates** is manager-only. In **Format Studio**, select the season competition, division, finalized participant set, and format settings. Use **Check Feasibility**, then **Generate Fixtures**. For multi-stage structures use **Validate Stage Graph**. Once accepted, use **Freeze Structure**.\n\n**Recovery:** Before freeze, correct and regenerate. Once operational matches or frozen stage snapshots exist, create a new structure version. The current UI has no generic structure-unfreeze command.\n\n### 6. Prepare and progress stages\n\nThere is no standalone **Stage Progression** menu. Stage controls are on the structure opened from:\n\n```text\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Format Studio\n```\n\nAvailable stage actions are **Prepare**, **Start**, and **Freeze & Progress**.\n\n**Recovery:** Correct a stage before starting it. Before progression, correct and recompute standings. Frozen snapshot lines are immutable; after progression use a corrected successor structure rather than editing evidence.\n\n### 7. Plan calendar capacity\n\nMenu:\n\n```text\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Calendar Planner\n```\n\nCreate match days, slot windows, and capacity, then use **Mark Capacity Ready**. Venue setup and constraints are under:\n\n```text\nFederation > Match Day > Venues\n```\n\n**Recovery:** Edit draft calendar records. If a schedule already uses the calendar, make the deliberate calendar correction and refresh the working schedule. Stale assignments are flagged, not silently deleted.\n\n### 8. Build the working schedule\n\nMenu:\n\n```text\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Schedule Planner\n```\n\nUse **Refresh Calendar Fixtures**. Assign dates, times, venues, and courts manually, or use **Auto-schedule**, **Preview**, and **Apply Auto-schedule**. Review fairness warnings.\n\n**Recovery:** Draft and change-requested schedules remain editable. A preview does not change the schedule until applied. Refresh fixtures, move assignments, or regenerate while mutable.\n\n### 9. Submit and review the schedule\n\nPlanner menu:\n\n```text\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Schedule Planner\n```\n\nUse **Submit for Review**. Approver menu:\n\n```text\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Schedule Review Queue\n```\n\nThe review exposes **Withdraw Submission**, **Request Changes**, and **Approve Schedule** according to state and role.\n\n**Recovery:** Withdraw a pending submission to return it to editing. Request changes to return it to the planner. If an approval is mistaken, do not publish; correct it through a new review cycle while preserving separation of duties.\n\n### 10. Publish the schedule\n\nUse **Publish Schedule** on an approved review. The two real publication menus are:\n\n```text\nFederation > Publication > Approved Schedules\nFederation > Publication > Schedule Publications\n```\n\n**Recovery:** Live publications are immutable. Open the published schedule under **Federation > Competition Workflow > Schedule Planner**, use **Amend Schedule**, enter a reason, and press **Create Amendment**. The replacement follows submit, review, approve, and publish again. The old publication becomes superseded. There is no in-place **Unpublish** button.\n\n### 11. Operate match day\n\nMenu:\n\n```text\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Match-Day Control\n```\n\nUse **Open Match Day** when ready. During operations use **Update Court**, **Report Incident**, or **Operational Schedule Change**. Finish with **Close Match Day**. Supporting records are under:\n\n```text\nFederation > Match Day > Match Sheets\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Technical Records > Incidents\nFederation > Competition Workflow > Technical Records > Operational Deviations\n```\n\n**Recovery:** Resolve incident and deviation records with **Resolve**. Operational changes are recorded rather than silently altering the approved publication. Correct the planning baseline through a schedule amendment.\n\n### 12. Submit and approve results\n\nBackend menu:\n\n```text\nFederation > Planning > Advanced Records > Matches\n```\n\nResult Control extends the match form; there is no separate backend **Results** menu. The form provides **Submit Result**, **Verify Result**, **Approve Result**, **Raise Dispute / Request Exception**, **Correct Result**, and **Reset to Draft**.\n\nClub portal:\n\n```text\nMy Account > Results\n```\n\n**Recovery:** Reset an incorrect in-progress result to draft. Raise a dispute for governed review. Correct contested or approved results through **Correct Result**. Never silently edit an approved score.\n\n### 13. Recompute and freeze standings\n\nMenu:\n\n```text\nFederation > Publication > Standings\n```\n\nUse **Recompute** or **Queue Recompute**, inspect lines and tie-break notes, and then use **Freeze**.\n\n**Recovery:** Recompute while unfrozen. For a frozen standing, use **Unfreeze**, correct the source result or rule, use **Force Recompute**, and freeze again. Failed asynchronous work appears under the nested **Recompute Queue**.\n\n### 14. Monitor exceptions\n\nMenus:\n\n```text\nFederation > Planning > Operational Action Queue\nFederation > Administration > Reporting > Operational Health\nFederation > Administration > Operational Job Health\n```\n\nUse source links to correct business records in their owning workflow. Retry only retryable jobs.\n\n## Required terminology in operator documentation\n\n- Use **Federation > Competition Workflow**, not “Competition Engine”.\n- Use **Registration Desk**, not “Registration Windows” as a menu.\n- Use **Schedule Planner**, not “Scheduling > Schedules”.\n- Use **Schedule Review Queue**, not “Schedule Approval > Reviews”.\n- Use **Publication > Schedule Publications**, not “Schedule Approval > Publications”.\n- Use **Planning > Advanced Records > Matches**, not “Results > Match Results”.\n- Use **Publication > Standings**, not “Standings > Standings”.\n- Use **Format Studio** for stage progression; there is no standalone progression menu.\n", "content_length": 10699, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "docs/COMPETITION_UI_WORKFLOW.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "cd08e472281fa27aa4999aa4f0206a44e569cde6e1abcb981f0b34229033f40a", "size_bytes": 11089}
 {"content": "# Delivery Roadmap\n\nOwner: Federation Platform Team\nLast updated: 2026-09-02\nReview cadence: Every release\n\n## Purpose\n\nThis roadmap turns the stabilized competition architecture into an executable\nsequence of release, product, reliability, operations, and usability milestones.\nThe ownership chain remains unchanged:\n\n`registration → format → calendar → scheduling → schedule approval → match day`\n\nPublished schedules remain immutable, role separation remains enforced, and\nportal/public access continues through explicit ownership boundaries.\n\n## Release Baseline Qualification\n\nStatus: **Implemented, pending execution on the committed candidate**\n\n### Objective\n\nCreate one reproducible candidate commit for which static validation, fresh\ninstallation, focused release surfaces, performance qualification, browser\nfocus tests, and the full standard suite all pass.\n\n### Automation\n\nRun after committing all functional changes:\n\n```bash\nscripts/ci/run_release_baseline.sh\n```\n\nThe script executes:\n\n1. clean-workspace preflight;\n2. static and contract validation;\n3. fresh installation and standard tests;\n4. core qualification;\n5. portal qualification;\n6. public-site qualification;\n7. performance qualification;\n8. browser and release-focus qualification;\n9. final full standard suite;\n10. machine-readable evidence capture per lane.\n\nEvidence is written under `artifacts/release/baseline/` by default. Override this\nwith `RELEASE_BASELINE_EVIDENCE_DIR` when CI provides a dedicated artifact\ndirectory.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\n- One immutable candidate SHA is recorded in every evidence file.\n- Test failures and errors are zero.\n- Static, ownership, workflow, migration, publication, and security contracts pass.\n- Performance budgets pass.\n- Browser lifecycle, finance, and public-site tours pass.\n- Local and GitHub release-candidate validation agree on the same commit.\n- The worktree is clean before qualification starts.\n\n## Release Candidate and Migration Evidence\n\nStatus: **Implemented, pending rehearsal with an approved backup**\n\n### Objective\n\nProve deployment and recovery against a representative pre-refactor database and\nmatching filestore.\n\n### Automation\n\nRun the complete migration rehearsal from a clean candidate checkout:\n\n```bash\nscripts/ci/run_migration_rehearsal.sh \\\n  --backup-dir /path/to/approved-backup \\\n  --rollback-owner \"Release owner\" \\\n  --rollback-trigger \"Invariant comparison or acceptance validation fails\" \\\n  --yes\n```\n\nThe command restores the source backup, captures pre-upgrade invariants, upgrades\nthe restored database, captures post-upgrade invariants, runs role-separated\nacceptance, restores the same backup into an independent rollback database, and\ncompares all evidence. Evidence is written under\n`artifacts/release/migration/` by default.\n\n### Deliverables\n\n- production-like database and filestore restore;\n- pre-upgrade invariant snapshot;\n- reviewed module upgrade;\n- post-upgrade invariant comparison;\n- role-separated operator pilot;\n- backup checksum and restore evidence;\n- rollback drill with named owner and trigger;\n- archived RC logs and machine-readable evidence.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\nThe upgraded copy preserves competition, registration, fixture, schedule,\npublication, match-day, result, standings, portal, public, integration, and\nattachment invariants. Restore and rollback complete from the recorded backup.\n\n## Simplified Competition Journey\n\nStatus: **Implemented**\n\n### Objective\n\nAllow a normal league, tournament day, or cup to be configured, scheduled,\napproved, and published without exposing the underlying Odoo model structure.\n\n### Deliverables\n\n- template-led competition creation;\n- guided Basics, Participants, Format, Calendar, Schedule, Publish journey;\n- user-facing Draft, Ready, Live, Finished presentation states;\n- visible blockers, responsible role, and next action;\n- advanced stage graph, revision, fairness, and shared-day controls separated\n  from the normal path;\n- task-first club portal.\n\n### Reversibility contract\n\n- Setup templates apply editable defaults and can be cancelled.\n- Late fixtures can be added to or removed from calendar allocations.\n- Draft and change-requested schedules refresh from the current calendar plan without deleting assignments.\n- Submitting planners can withdraw pending reviews; approver-requested changes return the same schedule to an editable state.\n- Published evidence stays immutable and corrections use a linked replacement revision.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\nA trained non-developer completes the normal journey without developer mode, direct model navigation, manual progression mapping, or shell access. Every pre-publication handoff has an explicit return path; post-publication changes use traceable replacement revisions.\n\n## Operational Job Reliability\n\nStatus: **Implemented**\n\n### Objective\n\nGeneralize the proven standings recompute reliability pattern without moving\ndomain execution out of its owning addon.\n\n### Deliverables\n\n- bounded retry and backoff;\n- stale-running recovery;\n- dead-letter/operator-action state;\n- correlation IDs and sanitized error evidence;\n- idempotent manual retry;\n- operator visibility for notification, report, integration, retention, and\n  snapshot jobs;\n- shared infrastructure only after two proven consumers exist.\n\n### Implementation\n\nThe shared `federation.operation.job` queue records source ownership, attempts,\nretry timing, correlation IDs, stale-worker recovery, terminal operator action,\nand manual retry. Notification and scheduled-report failures can be queued for\nautomatic retry. Failed inbound deliveries create operator-action jobs because\npayload and governance corrections require explicit review before replay.\n\nThe **Operational Job Health** administration view groups work by state and\nsource, exposes sanitized failure evidence, opens the source record, and permits\nexplicit retry only for failed jobs.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\nOperators can identify what failed, whether it will retry, when it will retry,\nhow many attempts remain, which correlation ID applies, and whether manual retry\nis safe.\n\n## Retention Evidence and Recovery Visibility\n\nStatus: **Implemented**\n\n### Objective\n\nTurn retention execution into auditable operational evidence and surface\nrecovery readiness in one place.\n\n### Deliverables\n\n- candidate, deleted, skipped, attachment, and failure counts;\n- duration, policy window/version, dry-run flag, and correlation ID;\n- read-only retention evidence views;\n- overdue and repeated-failure alerts;\n- release-readiness checks for disabled or stale retention jobs;\n- links to restore and rollback evidence.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\nThe release owner can prove that each policy ran, used the intended window,\nretained unresolved workflow evidence, cleaned matching attachments, and exposed\nfailures to operators.\n\n## Consolidated Operational Dashboard\n\nStatus: **Implemented**\n\n### Objective\n\nAggregate existing release, job, retention, integration, and competition queues\nwithout duplicating their source records or commands.\n\n### Deliverables\n\n- release readiness and last evidence status;\n- queued, retrying, dead-letter, and stale job health;\n- retention freshness and outcomes;\n- failed or partial integration deliveries;\n- registration blockers, review backlog, unpublished approved schedules,\n  match-day publication gaps, result backlog, and standings failures;\n- direct links to owning records.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\nAn operator can assess release and live operational health from one dashboard and\nnavigate directly to the owning workflow for remediation.\n\n## Accessibility and Operator Usability\n\nStatus: **Implemented**\n\n### Objective\n\nQualify the guided journey and principal portal/public workflows for keyboard,\nmobile, terminology, and accessible error recovery.\n\n### Deliverables\n\n- keyboard-only competition setup acceptance;\n- modal focus and focus-restoration checks;\n- linked validation summaries;\n- non-color status cues and accessible icon labels;\n- mobile portal qualification;\n- raw internal-state prevention;\n- deterministic training sandbox and operator walkthrough.\n\n### Exit criteria\n\nThe release pilot is completable by keyboard, principal mobile portal workflows\nremain usable, and user-facing terminology consistently describes the simplified\njourney rather than internal model states.\n\n## Change Control\n\nEach milestone must ship as focused, test-backed changes with corresponding\nworkflow, module, technical, and runbook documentation. New abstractions require\nmultiple proven consumers. Security checks, ownership boundaries, publication\nimmutability, and release validation must not be weakened to accommodate\nimplementation drift.\n", "content_length": 8675, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "docs/DELIVERY_ROADMAP.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "42be6fb8c7e87798d70090bf3701f695b00991e74f043c8110615509842c8fc1", "size_bytes": 8685}

 {"content": "# Deterministic Release Pilot Scenario\n\nOwner: Federation Platform Team\nReview cadence: Every release candidate\n\nThis scenario is the P3 operational acceptance path. It complements module tests\nby proving that one federation storyline can be followed across ownership\nboundaries without hidden setup or direct database edits.\n\n## Automated gate\n\nRun the complete release-focus verification:\n\n```bash\npython3 ci/check_release_focus_contract.py\nscripts/ci/run_rc_validation.sh focus\n```\n\nThe focus lane runs the lifecycle, finance, and public browser tours together\nwith the schedule-amendment, approval immutability, officiating, governance,\nportal-security, compliance, and pilot-readiness tests.\n\n## Pilot storyline\n\n1. Create a season and competition edition.\n2. Open registration and finalize the participant set.\n3. Freeze the competition structure and generate fixtures.\n4. Prepare match-day capacity and assign every fixture to a slot.\n5. Submit the schedule for independent review and publish the approved revision.\n6. Assign named officials or a club officiating duty and resolve shortages.\n7. Open match-day control, record results, and approve official results.\n8. Recompute and publish standings.\n9. Confirm the public competition, schedule, format, and standings pages.\n10. Confirm finance-event creation, settlement, export readiness, and traceability.\n11. Amend one published schedule through a replacement revision and verify that\n    the original publication and audit evidence remain immutable.\n12. Verify that an unrelated portal user cannot read or mutate another club's\n    registration, roster, officiating, result, or compliance records.\n\n## Acceptance evidence\n\nRecord the release-candidate commit, database name, commands, start/end time,\nall automated results, and any manual observations in the release runbook. A\nrelease candidate is not accepted while any focus-lane test is skipped, flaky,\nor dependent on manual data repair.\n\n## Role-separated acceptance gate\n\nRun the operator-role contract before the browser pilot:\n\n```bash\nscripts/ci/run_rc_validation.sh focus\n```\n\nThe pilot must use distinct registration, format, calendar, schedule-planning, schedule-approval, and match-day operators. Planning and approval must not be collapsed into the administrator account. The browser tour remains the navigation smoke test; owning addon tests remain authoritative for state transitions and negative workflow decisions.\n\n\n## UI navigation\n\nUse [`COMPETITION_UI_WORKFLOW.md`](COMPETITION_UI_WORKFLOW.md) for verified backend and portal menu paths, state actions, and recovery procedures.\n", "content_length": 2613, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "docs/RELEASE_PILOT_SCENARIO.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "ce8864d3e61be962f21126be5ea277001dc11c7dd951ccfc88fb93e664769fa0", "size_bytes": 2613}

 {"content": "# Sports Federation Club Roles and Portal\n\nVersion: 19.0.4.1.0\nOwner: Federation Platform Team\nLast reviewed: 2026-08-23\nReview cadence: Every release\n\nRole-oriented self-service portal for club representatives, officials, and tournament operators.\n\n## Primary user journeys\n\n### Club representative\n\n- manage club teams and players\n- submit season and tournament registrations\n- prepare and activate rosters\n- complete match sheets and readiness tasks\n- nominate officials\n- review result actions and deadlines\n\n### Official\n\n- view assignments\n- confirm or decline within the authorized scope\n- access relevant match information\n\n### Tournament operator\n\n- use the tournament operations board\n- monitor courts, matches, readiness, and action queues\n- execute scoped result and operational actions\n\n## Trust boundary\n\nAll privileged portal writes must use a model-owned method and the shared `federation.portal.privilege` boundary. Controllers validate input and resolve scope but do not perform raw elevated business writes. See `adr/0001-portal-trust-boundaries.md`.\n\nEvery new privileged route requires:\n\n1. positive in-scope coverage\n2. negative out-of-scope coverage\n3. direct-ID or guessed-record coverage\n4. model/helper ownership assertions when elevation is used\n5. CSRF enforcement for state-changing browser or JSON-RPC routes\n\n## Portal UX direction\n\nThe landing page should remain task-first. Common tasks are teams, registrations, upcoming matches, and action items. Specialist tools should be progressively disclosed instead of presented as an undifferentiated module catalogue.\n\n## Operations board\n\nThe board loads through scoped JSON-RPC, supports polling and structured failures, and exposes only matches and actions available to the current user. Server-side checks remain authoritative.\n\n## Tests\n\nThe module includes HTTP smoke, ownership, access-denied, team, player, roster, registration, officiating, result, accessibility, mobile, operations-board, and end-to-end portal workflow tests.\n\n## Consolidated documentation\n\n`PORTAL_OWNERSHIP_COVERAGE.md`, `PORTAL_OWNERSHIP_TEST_MATRIX.md`, and `ROADMAP_RC.md` are deleted. Their durable rules are consolidated here and in ADR-0001; the test files are the authoritative live coverage map.\n\n## competition portal\n\nThe active competition portal is edition-based. Participation comes from\ncompetition entries and finalized participant sets. Match-day schedule data is\nrendered only from each match day's current live publication. Draft schedules,\nreview evidence and superseded publications are intentionally unavailable.\n\nPrimary routes:\n\n- `/my/competitions`\n- `/my/competition-entries`\n- `/my/match-days`\n- `/sports/match-days/<id>/operations`\n\nLegacy tournament workspace routes are redirect-only and render no V1 data.\nThe backend tournament-registration action is loaded before the menus that use\nit, so a fresh module install and an upgrade both build the registration menu\nwithout relying on stale database metadata.\n\n\n## Accessible error recovery and focus management\n\nPortal forms move keyboard focus to server-rendered error summaries, connect\ninvalid controls to that summary with `aria-describedby`, and announce the\nsummary through an assertive live region. Bootstrap confirmation dialogs move\nfocus into the dialog and restore it to the invoking control when closed.\nKeyboard focus uses a visible outline that does not depend on color-only status\ncues. The focused accessibility suite covers these contracts together with\nmobile table and action wrapping.\n\n\n## Club competition data tutorial\n\nClub representatives use **My Account > Data API** (`/my/data-api`) for live URLs, curl and JavaScript examples, privacy rules, and caching guidance.\n", "content_length": 3727, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_portal/README.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "20882b3c9483b628db1e4dd12c84940bfd66b2b4f9416ade24df2df785f0e97f", "size_bytes": 3727}

 {"content": "import re\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom lxml import etree\n\nfrom odoo.tests.common import TransactionCase, tagged\n\nMIN_COLUMNS_FOR_RESPONSIVE = 5\n\n\n@tagged(\"-at_install\", \"post_install\", \"sf_frontend_mobile\")\nclass TestPortalTemplateMobile(TransactionCase):\n    \"\"\"Verify mobile-specific layout conventions in the largest portal templates.\n\n    These checks complement the accessibility tests by validating that wide\n    tables are horizontally scrollable, that action-button groups wrap on\n    narrow viewports, and that no inline width constraints cause horizontal\n    overflow on phone-sized screens.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls):\n        super().setUpClass()\n        views_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / \"views\"\n        cls.template_roots = {\n            \"portal_templates\": etree.parse(\n                str(views_dir / \"portal_templates.xml\")\n            ).getroot(),\n            \"portal_roster_templates\": etree.parse(\n                str(views_dir / \"portal_roster_templates.xml\")\n            ).getroot(),\n            \"competition_templates\": etree.parse(\n                str(views_dir / \"competition_templates.xml\")\n            ).getroot(),\n            \"portal_officiating_templates\": etree.parse(\n                str(views_dir / \"portal_officiating_templates.xml\")\n            ).getroot(),\n        }\n\n    def _template(self, file_key, template_id):\n        templates = self.template_roots[file_key].xpath(\n            f\".//template[@id='{template_id}']\"\n        )\n        self.assertTrue(\n            templates, f\"Template {template_id} should exist in {file_key}.\"\n        )\n        return templates[0]\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Assertion helpers\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _assert_wide_tables_wrapped_responsive(self, file_key, template_id):\n        \"\"\"Tables with MIN_COLUMNS_FOR_RESPONSIVE+ header columns must live\n        inside a ``table-responsive`` ancestor so they scroll on narrow screens.\n        \"\"\"\n        template = self._template(file_key, template_id)\n        tables = template.xpath(\".//table[.//thead]\")\n        for table in tables:\n            col_count = len(table.xpath(\".//thead//th\"))\n            if col_count < MIN_COLUMNS_FOR_RESPONSIVE:\n                continue\n            # Walk up to find a table-responsive wrapper\n            parent = table.getparent()\n            found = False\n            while parent is not None:\n                classes = parent.get(\"class\", \"\")\n                if \"table-responsive\" in classes.split():\n                    found = True\n                    break\n                parent = parent.getparent()\n            self.assertTrue(\n                found,\n                f\"Table with {col_count} columns in {template_id} \"\n                f\"should be wrapped in a table-responsive container.\",\n            )\n\n    def _assert_action_groups_wrap(self, file_key, template_id):\n        \"\"\"Button groups using ``d-flex`` should include ``flex-wrap`` so\n        action buttons stack on phone-width viewports instead of overflowing.\n        \"\"\"\n        template = self._template(file_key, template_id)\n        groups = template.xpath(\n            \".//*[contains(concat(' ', normalize-space(@class), ' '), ' d-flex ')]\"\n        )\n        for group in groups:\n            classes = group.get(\"class\", \"\").split()\n            if \"d-flex\" not in classes:\n                continue\n            # Only check groups that contain buttons or links\n            has_actions = group.xpath(\"./*[self::button or self::a or self::form]\")\n            if not has_actions:\n                continue\n            self.assertIn(\n                \"flex-wrap\",\n                classes,\n                f\"Action group in {template_id} uses d-flex but is missing \"\n                f\"flex-wrap for mobile overflow.\",\n            )\n\n    def _assert_no_table_fixed_width_overflow(self, file_key, template_id):\n        \"\"\"Data tables (with <thead>) should not carry inline ``width`` styles\n        on the <table> element itself, as this can force horizontal overflow\n        on narrow viewports.\n        \"\"\"\n        template = self._template(file_key, template_id)\n        tables = template.xpath(\".//table[.//thead]\")\n        for table in tables:\n            style = table.get(\"style\", \"\")\n            self.assertFalse(\n                re.search(r\"width\\s*:\", style),\n                f\"Data table in {template_id} has an inline width style \"\n                f\"that may cause mobile overflow.\",\n            )\n\n    # -{"content": "# Sports Federation Public Site\n\nPublic website coverage for tournaments, now integrated into the main\nTournaments navigation instead of a separate Competitions surface.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThis module turns published tournaments into a coherent public product for fans,\nclubs, and media. It provides a tournament hub, overview pages, schedule and\nresults pages, standings snapshots, team profile pages, JSON feed access, and\ncalendar export while keeping older competition URLs working.\n\n## Dependencies\n\n| Module | Reason |\n|--------|--------|\n| `website` | Odoo website framework |\n| `sports_federation_portal` | Main tournament website hub and navigation |\n| `sports_federation_tournament` | Tournaments, matches, participants |\n| `sports_federation_standings` | Standings data |\n| `sports_federation_venues` | Venue information |\n| `sports_federation_result_control` | Approved result visibility |\n\n## Model extensions\n\n### `federation.tournament`\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `website_published` | Boolean | Enables public visibility |\n| `public_slug` | Char | Legacy public slug retained for tournament feed and redirect compatibility |\n| `public_description` | Html | Long-form website description |\n| `public_featured` | Boolean | Marks the tournament for featured hub sections |\n| `public_editorial_summary` | Text | Short editorial summary for cards and overview layouts |\n| `public_pinned_announcement` | Text | Announcement banner content for the overview page |\n| `public_hero_image` | Binary | Hero image for the public overview page |\n| `show_public_results` | Boolean | Enables the public results page |\n| `show_public_standings` | Boolean | Enables the public standings page |\n\nKey helper methods expose canonical public URLs, published tournament queries,\nteam/result/standings/schedule datasets, JSON feed payloads, and ICS calendar\nexport content.\n\n### `federation.team`\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `public_slug` | Char | Canonical public slug used in `/teams/<slug>` |\n\nTeam helpers provide stable team profile URLs and public tournament links.\nTheir public slug resolver accepts an extra publication domain so controller\nlookups can fail closed before rendering profile, follow, feed, or calendar\nsurfaces.\n\n### `federation.season`\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `website_published` | Boolean | Enables season-wide discovery on the website |\n| `public_slug` | Char | Canonical public slug used in `/seasons/<slug>` |\n| `public_summary` | Text | Short summary used on season discovery pages |\n| `public_description` | Html | Long-form season landing page content |\n\nSeason helpers provide canonical season URLs, featured and recent tournament\nqueries, editorial aggregation for season landing pages, and fail-closed public\nslug resolution for published-season routes.\n\n### `federation.public.editorial.item`\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `publication_state` | Selection | draft / scheduled / published / archived |\n| `content_type` | Selection | highlight / announcement / update |\n| `publish_start` / `publish_end` | Datetime | Optional publication window |\n| `season_id` / `tournament_id` / `team_id` | Many2one | Public anchor for the item |\n| `summary` | Text | Card-friendly editorial summary |\n| `body_html` | Html | Long-form editorial content |\n\nEditorial items let operators schedule season-, tournament-, or team-linked\nhighlights without editing website templates. Scheduling requires a publish\nstart while the item is still in draft; items can publish from draft or\nscheduled, archive from scheduled or published, and reset from scheduled or\narchived back to draft.\n\n### `federation.standing`\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `website_published` | Boolean | Allows standings publication |\n| `public_title` | Char | Public-facing table title |\n\n## Controllers\n\n### `PublicTournamentHubController`\n\nCanonical public routes use one namespace only:\n\n| Route | Auth | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `GET /competitions` | public | Competition hub |\n| `POST /competitions/api/json` | public | Published competition discovery |\n| `GET /competitions/<edition-slug>` | public | Competition overview with published divisions |\n| `GET /competitions/<edition-slug>/format` | public | Published format and bracket information |\n| `GET /competitions/<edition-slug>/schedule` | public | Published competition schedule |\n| `GET /competitions/<edition-slug>/gamedays/<matchday-id>` | public | Published game-day board |\n| `GET /competitions/<edition-slug>/register?division_id=<division-id>` | user | Club registration form |\n| `POST /competitions/<edition-slug>/register?division_id=<division-id>` | user | Club registration submission |\n| `GET /competitions/<edition-slug>/divisions/<division-slug>/schedule.ics` | public | Division calendar export |\n| `GET /api/v1/competitions` | public | Published competition API index |\n| `GET /api/v1/competitions/<edition-slug>` | public | Complete published competition feed |\n| `GET /teams/<slug>` | public | Public team profile |\n\nThere are no tournament-named or numeric compatibility routes. The product had not been released when the namespace was consolidated, so only `/competitions` is supported.\n\n### `PublicFollowController`\n\nCanonical follow and discovery routes:\n\n| Route | Auth | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `GET /seasons` | public | Season discovery page for published seasons |\n| `GET /seasons/<slug>` | public | Canonical season landing page with editorial and tournament sections |\n| `GET /teams/<slug>/schedule` | public | Team-centric grouped upcoming schedule |\n| `GET /teams/<slug>/results` | public | Team-centric recent approved results |\n| `GET /teams/<slug>/schedule.ics` | public | Team schedule calendar export |\n| `GET /api/v1/teams/<slug>/feed` | public | Stable v1 team follow feed |\n\n## Public-site behaviour\n\n1. Tournament-first navigation: the visible website now centers tournaments and nests published coverage under the existing Tournaments area.\n2. Slug-first URLs: tournaments and teams use canonical slug routes, with numeric routes preserved only for backward compatibility.\n3. Editorial control: operators can feature tournaments, add summaries, pin announcements, and assign hero imagery without editing templates.\n4. Better hub discovery: the main hub separates featured, live, recent, and archived tournaments instead of showing one flat list.\n5. Team-aware public pages: standings, results, schedule, and teams pages link through to public team profiles when available.\n6. Sticky section navigation: tournament sub-pages share one consistent local navigation model.\n7. Calendar export: schedules can be consumed as `.ics` for personal or club calendars.\n8. Stable feed contract: the v1 feed includes slug-aware public URLs alongside tournament, standings, schedule, bracket, and result data.\n9. Visibility enforcement: results and standings respect per-tournament publication toggles.\n10. Sanitized website rendering: public descriptions and website content render through standard website field handling.\n11. Backward compatibility: legacy competition routes keep resolving while the visible product language is tournament-first.\n12. Season discovery and editorial planning: published seasons aggregate featured tournaments, recent tournaments, and live editorial items with publication windows.\n13. Automatic menu cleanup: module installs and upgrades normalize stale `/competitions` website menus into the Tournaments submenu and remove leftover duplicates.\n14. Shared privileged-write boundary: website registration POST routes validate HTTP input and then reuse the same tournament registration helper as the portal layer.\n15. Publication handoff guidance: tournament and standings Website tabs now explain whether visibility is blocked by tournament publication, missing standings, unpublished standings, or disabled standings toggles, with direct links back to standings when operators need to act there.\n\n## Publication guards\n\n- Controllers resolve tournaments, seasons, and team public routes through publication- and visibility-scoped queries before canonical redirects, page rendering, feed reads, or registration handling.\n- Unpublished tournaments lose public access immediately, regardless of related data still present in the database.\n- Legacy numeric compatibility routes redirect only while the tournament still satisfies the relevant public visibility guard.\n- Season slug and numeric detail routes only resolve website-published seasons.\n- Team profile, schedule, results, ICS, and feed routes only resolve teams with a published competition footprint.\n- Public results are limited to approved results.\n- Public participant lists exclude withdrawn entries.\n- Standings exposure depends on both tournament publication and the standings visibility controls.\n\n\n## Public controller boundaries\n\nRoute orchestration lives in `public_competitions.py`. Framework response, canonical resolution, authenticated club lookup, error redirect, and rate-limit response behavior live in `_public_request.py`. New domain queries belong in model query services rather than either controller helper.\n\n\n## Complete competition API\n\n`GET /api/v1/competitions` lists published competitions; `GET /api/v1/competitions/<edition-slug>` aggregates every published division and its public teams, schedules, approved results, standings, brackets, and links.\n", "content_length": 9558, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_public_site/README.md", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "4e66aab2eb5740f405ed046ccc7a7c2183de0973d1b3fddcb59b9655dda911ba", "size_bytes": 9558}

 {"content": "\"\"\"Filter and domain-building helpers for public tournament hub routes.\n\nExtracted from ``PublicTournamentHubController`` so that the main controller\nmodule stays focused on routing and rendering.  Mix this class in first when\ncomposing the controller.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom odoo.http import request\n\n\nclass TournamentHubFilterMixin:\n    \"\"\"Mixin that provides filter parsing and domain building for hub routes.\"\"\"\n\n    # -- Request parameter parsing -------------------------------------------\n\n    def _parse_int_param(self, value):\n        \"\"\"Parse int param.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            return int(value) if value else False\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n            return False\n\n    def _build_filters(self, search=\"\", **kw):\n        \"\"\"Build a normalised filter dict from inbound request parameters.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"search\": (search or kw.get(\"search\") or \"\").strip(),\n            \"season_id\": self._parse_int_param(kw.get(\"season_id\")),\n            \"state\": (kw.get(\"state\") or \"\").strip(),\n            \"category\": (kw.get(\"category\") or \"\").strip(),\n            \"gender\": (kw.get(\"gender\") or \"\").strip(),\n            \"venue_id\": self._parse_int_param(kw.get(\"venue_id\")),\n        }\n\n    # -- Domain builders ------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _build_shared_filter_domain(self, filters):\n        \"\"\"Return the ORM domain clauses that are shared by hub sections.\"\"\"\n        Tournament = request.env[\"federation.tournament\"]\n        domain = []\n        if filters[\"search\"]:\n            domain += Tournament._get_public_site_search_domain(filters[\"search\"])\n        if filters[\"season_id\"]:\n            domain.append((\"season_id\", \"=\", filters[\"season_id\"]))\n        if filters[\"category\"]:\n            domain.append((\"category\", \"=\", +## Competition route upgrade safety
+
+The `19.0.3.0.0` pre-migration repairs the stored selector of the competition-hub editorial extension before Odoo reloads its parent template. This prevents databases upgraded from the former route namespace from failing registry initialization while the canonical `/competitions` templates are loaded. Fresh installations already use the canonical selector and require no special action.

+====================================================================================================
+FILE: sports_federation_public_site/migrations/19.0.3.0.0/pre-migrate.py
+SIZE: 1009
+CONTENT-LENGTH: 1009
+SHA256: 0d55730668ceee9854d620863dbef47d02572a0ac7af120b9f4f66aec34a3ac9
+GIT-MODE: 100644
+FILESYSTEM-MODE: 0644
+GIT-STATE: tracked

+
+import logging
+
+
+_logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+
+_VIEW_MODULE = "sports_federation_public_site"
+_VIEW_NAME = "page_tournaments_hub_discovery_sections"
+_OLD_ACTION = "/" + "tournaments"
+_NEW_ACTION = "/competitions"
+
+
+def migrate(cr, version):
+    """Repair the stored inherited-view selector before parent views are reloaded."""
+    cr.execute(
+        """
+        UPDATE ir_ui_view AS view
+           SET arch_db = replace(view.arch_db::text, %s, %s)::jsonb
+          FROM ir_model_data AS data
+         WHERE data.module = %s
+           AND data.name = %s
+           AND data.model = 'ir.ui.view'
+           AND data.res_id = view.id
+           AND view.arch_db::text LIKE %s
+        """,
+        (
+            _OLD_ACTION,
+            _NEW_ACTION,
+            _VIEW_MODULE,
+            _VIEW_NAME,
+            f"%{_OLD_ACTION}%",
+        ),
+    )
+    if cr.rowcount:
+        _logger.info(
+            "Updated the stored competition-hub discovery selector to %s",
+            _NEW_ACTION,
+        )
